      Text50001@1000000002 : TextConst 'DEU=Der Suchcode darf nicht leer sein, keine Umlaute und keine Sonderzeichen enthalten';
      Text50002@1000000003 : TextConst 'DEU=Der Suchcode muss eindeutig sein';
      WebshopUpdateManagement@1000000004 : Codeunit 5447301;

    PROCEDURE GetCategoryPath@1000000001() CategoryPath : Text[1024];
    VAR
      i@1000000000 : Integer;
      Category@1000000001 : Record 5447303;
    BEGIN
      // Gibt den Pfad (Breadcrumb) der Kategorie zur�ck
      CategoryPath := Name;
      IF Level > 1 THEN BEGIN
        FOR i := 1 TO Level-1 DO BEGIN
          Category.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
          Category.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
          Category.SETRANGE("Line No.",0,"Line No.");
          Category.SETRANGE(Level,Level-i);
          IF Category.FINDLAST THEN BEGIN
            CategoryPath := Category.Name + ' > ' + CategoryPath;
          END;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE CheckSearchCode@1000000000(SearchCode@1000000000 : Text[20]) NewSearchCode : Text[20];
    VAR
      ShopCategory@1000000001 : Record 5447303;
    BEGIN
      // Pr�fen ob Suchcode g�ltig ist (Eindeutig, keine Sonderzeichen)
      IF WebshopManagement.CodeHasSpecialChars(SearchCode) THEN
        ERROR(Text50001);
      ShopCategory.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
      ShopCategory.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
      ShopCategory.SETFILTER("Line No.",'<>%1',"Line No.");
      IF ShopCategory.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
        IF UPPERCASE(ShopCategory."Search Code") = UPPERCASE(SearchCode) THEN BEGIN
          SearchCode := INCSTR(SearchCode);
          ShopCategory.FINDSET;
        END;
      UNTIL ShopCategory.NEXT = 0;
      NewSearchCode := SearchCode;
      IF SearchCode = '' THEN
        ERROR(Text50001);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetCategoryURL@1000000003() CategoryPath : Text[1024];
    VAR
      i@1000000000 : Integer;
      Category@1000000001 : Record 5447303;
      WebshopManagement@1000000002 : Codeunit 5447300;
    BEGIN
      // Gibt den Pfad (Breadcrumb) der Kategorie zur�ck
      CategoryPath := '/' + WebshopManagement.URLEncodeReadable("Search Code") + '/';
      IF Level > 1 THEN BEGIN
        FOR i := 1 TO Level-1 DO BEGIN
          Category.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
          Category.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
          Category.SETRANGE("Line No.",0,"Line No.");
          Category.SETRANGE(Level,Level-i);
          IF Category.FINDLAST THEN BEGIN
            CategoryPath := '/' + WebshopManagement.URLEncodeReadable(Category."Search Code") + CategoryPath;
          END;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE HasChildren@1000000002() HasChildren : Boolean;
    VAR
      TempWebshopCategory@1000000000 : Record 5447303;
    BEGIN
      HasChildren := FALSE;
      TempWebshopCategory.SETFILTER("Line No.",'>%1',"Line No.");
      TempWebshopCategory.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
      TempWebshopCategory.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
      IF TempWebshopCategory.FINDFIRST THEN
        IF TempWebshopCategory.Level > Level THEN
          HasChildren := TRUE;
    END;

    PROCEDURE ModifyChildren@1000000005();
    VAR
      TempWebshopCategory@1000000000 : Record 5447303;
    BEGIN

      TempWebshopCategory.SETFILTER("Line No.",'>%1',"Line No.");
      TempWebshopCategory.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
      TempWebshopCategory.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
      IF TempWebshopCategory.FINDFIRST THEN REPEAT
        IF TempWebshopCategory.Level > Level THEN BEGIN
          TempWebshopCategory."Last Date Modified" := CURRENTDATETIME;
          TempWebshopCategory.MODIFY(FALSE);
        END ELSE
          EXIT;
      UNTIL TempWebshopCategory.NEXT = 0;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetLastChild@1000000015() LineNo : Integer;
    VAR
      TempWebshopCategory@1000000000 : Record 5447303;
    BEGIN
      LineNo := 0;
      TempWebshopCategory.SETFILTER("Line No.",'>%1',"Line No.");
      TempWebshopCategory.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
      TempWebshopCategory.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
      IF TempWebshopCategory.FINDFIRST THEN REPEAT
        IF TempWebshopCategory.Level > Level THEN
          LineNo := TempWebshopCategory."Line No."
        ELSE
          EXIT;
      UNTIL TempWebshopCategory.NEXT = 0;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetFirstChild@1000000016() LineNo : Integer;
    VAR
      TempWebshopCategory@1000000000 : Record 5447303;
    BEGIN
      LineNo := 0;
      TempWebshopCategory.SETFILTER("Line No.",'>%1',"Line No.");
      TempWebshopCategory.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
      TempWebshopCategory.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
      IF TempWebshopCategory.FINDFIRST THEN
        IF TempWebshopCategory.Level > Level THEN
          LineNo := TempWebshopCategory."Line No.";
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetNextCategoryLineNo@1000000010(ShopCode@1000000000 : Code[30];ShopLanguageCode@1000000001 : Code[10];AboveCode@1000000002 : Text[20]) NewLineNo : Integer;
    VAR
      AboveCategory@1000000003 : Record 5447303;
      BelowCategory@1000000004 : Record 5447303;
      NewLineNoInterval@1000000005 : Integer;
      NewLineNoNotRounded@1000000006 : Decimal;
    BEGIN
      NewLineNoInterval := 10000; //10.000
      AboveCategory.SETRANGE("Shop Code",ShopCode);
      AboveCategory.SETRANGE("Language Code",ShopLanguageCode);
      IF AboveCode <> '' THEN
        AboveCategory.SETRANGE("Search Code",AboveCode);
      IF (AboveCategory.COUNT = 1) AND (AboveCategory.FINDFIRST) THEN BEGIN
        //BelowCategory.SETRANGE("Shop Code",ShopCode);
        //BelowCategory.SETRANGE("Language Code",ShopLanguageCode);
        //BelowCategory.SETFILTER("Line No.",'>%1',AboveCategory."Line No.");
        //IF BelowCategory.ISEMPTY THEN BEGIN
          NewLineNo := AboveCategory."Line No." + NewLineNoInterval;
        //END ELSE BEGIN
        //  BelowCategory.FINDFIRST;
        //  NewLineNoNotRounded := (AboveCategory."Line No." + ((BelowCategory."Line No." - AboveCategory."Line No.")/2));
        //  NewLineNo := ROUND(NewLineNoNotRounded,1);
        //END;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        NewLineNo := NewLineNoInterval;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetAboveCodeForParentCode@1000000009(ShopCode@1000000000 : Code[30];ShopLanguageCode@1000000001 : Code[10];ParentCode@1000000002 : Text[40]) : Text[40];
    VAR
      ParentCategory@1000000003 : Record 5447303;
      LastChildCategory@1000000004 : Record 5447303;
      NextParentSiblingCategory@1000000005 : Record 5447303;
      Error001@1000000006 : TextConst 'DEU=Es konnte keine Kategorie zu den angegebenen Daten gefunden werden.;ENU=No Category could be found for the given Data.';
      AboveCategory@1000000007 : Record 5447303;
      Error002@1000000008 : TextConst 'DEU=Die letzte der angegebenen Kategorie untergeordnete Kategorie konnte nicht ausgelesen werden.;ENU=The last child element of the specified category could not be read.';
      AboveCode@1000000009 : Text[40];
    BEGIN
      ParentCategory.SETRANGE("Shop Code",ShopCode);
      ParentCategory.SETRANGE("Language Code",ShopLanguageCode);
      ParentCategory.SETRANGE("Search Code",ParentCode);
      IF (ParentCategory.COUNT = 1) AND (ParentCategory.FINDFIRST) THEN BEGIN
        IF HasCategoryChildren(ParentCategory) THEN BEGIN
          AboveCategory.SETRANGE("Shop Code",ShopCode);
          AboveCategory.SETRANGE("Language Code",ShopLanguageCode);
          AboveCategory.SETRANGE(Level,ParentCategory.Level + 1);
          IF HasCategoryNextSibling(ParentCategory) THEN
            AboveCategory.SETFILTER("Line No.",'>%1&<%2',ParentCategory."Line No.",GetNextCategorySiblingLineNo(ParentCategory));
          IF AboveCategory.FINDLAST THEN BEGIN
            AboveCode := AboveCategory."Search Code";
            EXIT(AboveCode);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            ERROR(Error002);
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          AboveCode := ParentCategory."Search Code";
          EXIT(AboveCode);
        END;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        AboveCategory.SETRANGE("Shop Code",ShopCode);
        AboveCategory.SETRANGE("Language Code",ShopLanguageCode);
        AboveCategory.SETRANGE(Level,1);
        IF AboveCategory.FINDLAST THEN
          AboveCode := AboveCategory."Search Code"
        ELSE
          AboveCode := '';
        EXIT(AboveCode);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetNextCategorySiblingLineNo@1000000008(ParamCategory@1000000000 : Record 5447303) NextSiblingCategoryLineNo : Integer;
    VAR
      NextSiblingCategory@1000000001 : Record 5447303;
      Error001@1000000002 : TextConst 'DEU=Es konnte keine Kategorie zu den angegebenen Daten gefunden werden.;ENU=No Category could be found for the given Data.';
      Error002@1000000003 : TextConst 'DEU=Die angegebene Kategorie hat kein n�chstes Geschwisterelement.;ENU=The provided Category has no next sibling element.';
    BEGIN
      IF ParamCategory.GET(ParamCategory."Shop Code",ParamCategory."Language Code",ParamCategory."Line No.") THEN BEGIN
        NextSiblingCategory.SETRANGE("Shop Code",ParamCategory."Shop Code");
        NextSiblingCategory.SETRANGE("Language Code",ParamCategory."Language Code");
        NextSiblingCategory.SETRANGE(Level,ParamCategory.Level);
        NextSiblingCategory.SETFILTER("Line No.",'>%1',ParamCategory."Line No.");
        IF NextSiblingCategory.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
          NextSiblingCategoryLineNo := NextSiblingCategory."Line No.";
        END ELSE
          ERROR(Error002);
      END ELSE
        ERROR(Error001);
    END;

    PROCEDURE HasCategoryNextSibling@1000000007(ParamCategory@1000000000 : Record 5447303) HasNextSibling : Boolean;
    VAR
      NextSiblingCategory@1000000001 : Record 5447303;
      Error001@1000000002 : TextConst 'DEU=Es konnte keine Kategorie zu den angegebenen Daten gefunden werden.;ENU=No Category could be found for the given Data.';
      Error002@1000000003 : TextConst 'DEU=Die angegebene Kategorie hat kein n�chstes Geschwisterelement.;ENU=The provided Category has no next sibling element.';
    BEGIN
      IF ParamCategory.GET(ParamCategory."Shop Code",ParamCategory."Language Code",ParamCategory."Line No.") THEN BEGIN
        NextSiblingCategory.SETRANGE("Shop Code",ParamCategory."Shop Code");
        NextSiblingCategory.SETRANGE("Language Code",ParamCategory."Language Code");
        NextSiblingCategory.SETRANGE(Level,ParamCategory.Level);
        NextSiblingCategory.SETFILTER("Line No.",'>%1',ParamCategory."Line No.");
        HasNextSibling := (NOT NextSiblingCategory.ISEMPTY);
      END ELSE
        ERROR(Error001);
    END;

    PROCEDURE HasCategoryChildren@1000000004(ParentCategory@1000000000 : Record 5447303) HasChildren : Boolean;
    VAR
      Category@1000000001 : Record 5447303;
      Error001@1000000002 : TextConst 'DEU=Es konnte keine Kategorie zu den angegebenen Daten gefunden werden.;ENU=No Category could be found for the given Data.';
    BEGIN
      IF ParentCategory.GET(ParentCategory."Shop Code",ParentCategory."Language Code",ParentCategory."Line No.") THEN BEGIN
        Category.SETRANGE("Shop Code",ParentCategory."Shop Code");
        Category.SETRANGE("Language Code",ParentCategory."Language Code");
        Category.SETRANGE(Level,ParentCategory.Level + 1);
        IF HasCategoryNextSibling(ParentCategory) THEN
          Category.SETFILTER("Line No.",'>%1&<%2',ParentCategory."Line No.",GetNextCategorySiblingLineNo(ParentCategory))
        ELSE
          Category.SETFILTER("Line No.",'>%1',ParentCategory."Line No.");
        HasChildren := (NOT Category.ISEMPTY);
      END ELSE
        ERROR(Error001);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetLineNoForSearchCode@1000000006(ShopCode@1000000000 : Code[30];LanguageCode@1000000001 : Code[10];SearchCode@1000000003 : Text[20]) : Integer;
    VAR
      Category@1000000002 : Record 5447303;
      LineNo@1000000004 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      Category.SETRANGE("Shop Code",ShopCode);
      Category.SETRANGE("Language Code",LanguageCode);
      Category.SETRANGE("Search Code",SearchCode);
      IF (Category.COUNT = 1) AND Category.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
        LineNo := Category."Line No.";
        EXIT(Category."Line No.");
      END ELSE BEGIN
        EXIT(0);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SwapCategoryTable@1000000012(ToCategory@1000000001 : Record 5447303;FromCategory@1000000017 : Record 5447303);
    VAR
      TempCategory@1000000008 : Record 5447303;
      TempItemCategory@1000000006 : Record 5447305;
      SwapToCategory@1000000005 : TEMPORARY Record 5447303;
      SwapFromCategory@1000000004 : TEMPORARY Record 5447303;
      SwapToItemCategory@1000000003 : TEMPORARY Record 5447305;
      SwapFromItemCategory@1000000002 : TEMPORARY Record 5447305;
      SwapFromWebshopItem@1000000009 : TEMPORARY Record 5447304;
      SwapToWebshopItem@1000000010 : TEMPORARY Record 5447304;
      WebshopItem@1000000011 : Record 5447304;
      SwapFromAttributeLink@1000000012 : TEMPORARY Record 5447321;
      SwapToAttributeLink@1000000013 : TEMPORARY Record 5447321;
      AttributeLink@1000000014 : Record 5447321;
      SwapToLineNoChildren@1000000015 : Integer;
      SwapFromLineNoChildren@1000000007 : Integer;
      SwapToLineNo@1000000000 : Integer;
      SwapFromLineNo@1000000016 : Integer;
      Shop@1000000019 : Record 5447301;
      Language@1000000018 : Record 5447302;
    BEGIN
      // Kategorien nach oben oder unter verschieben

      SwapFromLineNo := FromCategory."Line No.";
      SwapToLineNo := ToCategory."Line No.";

      //Artikel-Hauptkategorien �ndern
      WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Shop Code",FromCategory."Shop Code");
      WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Language Code",FromCategory."Language Code");
      WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Main Category Line No.",SwapFromLineNo);
      IF WebshopItem.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
        SwapFromWebshopItem.INIT;
        SwapFromWebshopItem.TRANSFERFIELDS(WebshopItem);
        SwapFromWebshopItem."Main Category Line No." := SwapToLineNo;
        SwapFromWebshopItem.INSERT(FALSE);
      UNTIL WebshopItem.NEXT = 0;

      WebshopItem.INIT;
      WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Shop Code",FromCategory."Shop Code");
      WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Language Code",FromCategory."Language Code");
      WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Main Category Line No.",SwapToLineNo);
      IF WebshopItem.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
        SwapToWebshopItem.INIT;
        SwapToWebshopItem.TRANSFERFIELDS(WebshopItem);
        SwapToWebshopItem."Main Category Line No." := SwapFromLineNo;
        SwapToWebshopItem.INSERT(FALSE);
      UNTIL WebshopItem.NEXT = 0;

      IF SwapFromWebshopItem.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
      IF WebshopItem.GET(SwapFromWebshopItem."Shop Code",SwapFromWebshopItem."Language Code",SwapFromWebshopItem."Item No.") THEN BEGIN
        WebshopItem."Main Category Line No." := SwapFromWebshopItem."Main Category Line No.";
        WebshopItem.MODIFY(TRUE);
      END;
      UNTIL SwapFromWebshopItem.NEXT = 0;

      IF SwapToWebshopItem.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
      IF WebshopItem.GET(SwapToWebshopItem."Shop Code",SwapToWebshopItem."Language Code",SwapToWebshopItem."Item No.") THEN BEGIN
        WebshopItem."Main Category Line No." := SwapToWebshopItem."Main Category Line No.";
        WebshopItem.MODIFY(TRUE);
      END;
      UNTIL SwapToWebshopItem.NEXT = 0;


      // Kateogorie-Attribute �ber temp. Tabellen verschieben
      AttributeLink.RESET;
      AttributeLink.SETRANGE(Type,AttributeLink.Type::Category);
      AttributeLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code",FromCategory."Shop Code");
      AttributeLink.SETRANGE("Language Code",FromCategory."Language Code");
      AttributeLink.SETRANGE("Category Line No.",SwapFromLineNo);
      IF AttributeLink.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
        SwapFromAttributeLink.INIT;
        SwapFromAttributeLink.TRANSFERFIELDS(AttributeLink);
        SwapFromAttributeLink."Category Line No." := SwapToLineNo;
        SwapFromAttributeLink.INSERT;
      UNTIL AttributeLink.NEXT = 0;

      AttributeLink.RESET;
      AttributeLink.SETRANGE(Type,AttributeLink.Type::Category);
      AttributeLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code",FromCategory."Shop Code");
      AttributeLink.SETRANGE("Language Code",FromCategory."Language Code");
      AttributeLink.SETRANGE("Category Line No.",SwapToLineNo);
      IF AttributeLink.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
        SwapToAttributeLink.INIT;
        SwapToAttributeLink.TRANSFERFIELDS(AttributeLink);
        SwapToAttributeLink."Category Line No." := SwapFromLineNo;
        SwapToAttributeLink.INSERT;
      UNTIL AttributeLink.NEXT = 0;

      AttributeLink.RESET;
      AttributeLink.SETRANGE(Type,AttributeLink.Type::Category);
      AttributeLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code",FromCategory."Shop Code");
      AttributeLink.SETRANGE("Language Code",FromCategory."Language Code");
      AttributeLink.SETFILTER("Category Line No.",'%1|%2',SwapFromLineNo,SwapToLineNo);
      AttributeLink.DELETEALL(FALSE);

      AttributeLink.RESET;
      IF SwapFromAttributeLink.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
        AttributeLink.INIT;
        AttributeLink.TRANSFERFIELDS(SwapFromAttributeLink);
        AttributeLink.INSERT;
      UNTIL SwapFromAttributeLink.NEXT = 0;

      AttributeLink.RESET;
      IF SwapToAttributeLink.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
        AttributeLink.INIT;
        AttributeLink.TRANSFERFIELDS(SwapToAttributeLink);
        AttributeLink.INSERT;
      UNTIL SwapToAttributeLink.NEXT = 0;

      COMMIT;
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- dynamic commerce - dynamic commerce GmbH - www.dc-solution.de ---
    }
    END.
  }
}

    PROCEDURE WebshopInventory@1000000000(VariantCode@1000000001 : Code[10]) WebshopInventory : Decimal;
    VAR
      Item@1000000000 : Record 27;
      BOMComponent@1000000006 : Record 90;
      BOMWebshopItem@1000000005 : Record 5447304;
      BOMInventory@1000000004 : Decimal;
      MinBOMInventory@1000000003 : Decimal;
      FirstBOMItem@1000000002 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      // Lagerbestand des Artikels berechnen
      Shop.GET("Shop Code");
      Item.GET("Item No.");

      Item.CALCFIELDS(Item."Bill of Materials");
      IF Item."Bill of Materials" THEN BEGIN
        MinBOMInventory := -1;
        BOMComponent.SETRANGE("Parent Item No.",Item."No.");
        BOMComponent.SETRANGE(Type,BOMComponent.Type::Item);
        FirstBOMItem := TRUE;
        IF BOMComponent.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
          BOMInventory := 0;

          IF BOMWebshopItem.GET("Shop Code","Language Code",BOMComponent."No.") AND (BOMComponent."Quantity per" <> 0) THEN BEGIN
            BOMInventory := BOMWebshopItem.WebshopInventory(BOMComponent."Variant Code");
            IF BOMInventory <= 0 THEN
              BOMInventory := 0;
            IF (BOMComponent."Quantity per" <> 1) AND (BOMInventory <> 0) THEN
              BOMInventory := ROUND(BOMInventory / BOMComponent."Quantity per",1,'<');
          END;

          IF FirstBOMItem OR (BOMInventory < MinBOMInventory) THEN
            MinBOMInventory := BOMInventory;
          FirstBOMItem := FALSE;
        UNTIL BOMComponent.NEXT = 0;
        WebshopInventory := MinBOMInventory;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        // Abh�ngig von Parameter auf Variante filtern
        IF VariantCode <> '' THEN
          Item.SETRANGE("Variant Filter",VariantCode);

        // Abh�ngig von Shop-Einrichtung Lagerortfilter ber�cksichtigen
        IF Shop."Location Code Filter" <> '' THEN
          Item.SETFILTER("Location Filter",Shop."Location Code Filter");

        // Lagerbestand aus Artikel berechnen
        Item.CALCFIELDS(Inventory);
        WebshopInventory := Item.Inventory;

        // Abh�ngig von Shop-Einrichtung Menge in Verkaufsauftrag abziehen
        IF Shop."Days Qty. on Sales Order" <> 0 THEN BEGIN
          Item.SETFILTER("Date Filter",'..' + FORMAT(CALCDATE('<CD+' + FORMAT(Shop."Days Qty. on Sales Order") + 'D>')));
          Item.CALCFIELDS("Qty. on Sales Order");
          WebshopInventory -= Item."Qty. on Sales Order";
        END;

        // Abh�ngig von Shop-Einrichtung  Menge in Einkaufsbestellung addieren
        IF Shop."Days Qty. on Purch Order" <> 0 THEN BEGIN
          Item.SETFILTER("Date Filter",'..' + FORMAT(CALCDATE('<CD+' + FORMAT(Shop."Days Qty. on Purch Order") + 'D>')));
          Item.CALCFIELDS("Qty. on Purch. Order");
          WebshopInventory += Item."Qty. on Purch. Order";
        END;
      END;

      IF WebshopInventory < 0 THEN
        WebshopInventory := 0;
    END;

    PROCEDURE WebshopRetailPrice@1000000001() RetailPrice : Decimal;
    VAR
      Item@1000000006 : Record 27;
      WebshopItem@1000000005 : Record 5447304;
      "Webshop Item Link"@1000000004 : Record 5447308;
      SalesPrice@1000000003 : Record 7002;
      VATPostingSetup@1000000007 : Record 325;
      AllowLineDisc@1000000002 : Boolean;
      AllCustPrice@1000000001 : Decimal;
      GroupPrice@1000000000 : Decimal;
    BEGIN
      // Ermittelt Webshop-Listenpreis anhand Shop-Einrichtung

      WITH Item DO BEGIN
        Shop.GET("Shop Code");
        IF GET("Item No.") THEN BEGIN
          WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE(Typ,WebshopItemLink.Typ::Variant);
          WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code",Shop.Code);
          WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE("Item No.","No.");
          IF (Shop."Variant Typ" = Shop."Variant Typ"::ShopVariantsParentItem)
          AND (WebshopItemLink.FINDSET) THEN BEGIN
            REPEAT
              WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Shop Code",Shop.Code);
              WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
              WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Item No.",WebshopItemLink."Linked Item No.");
              WebshopItem.SETRANGE(Active,TRUE);
              WebshopItem.SETFILTER(WebshopItem."Validity from",'%1 | ..%2', 0D, TODAY);
              WebshopItem.SETFILTER(WebshopItem."Validity to",'%1 | %2..', 0D, TODAY);
              IF WebshopItem.FINDFIRST THEN
                IF (RetailPrice > WebshopItem.WebshopBasePrice) OR (RetailPrice = 0) THEN
                  RetailPrice := WebshopItem.WebshopRetailPrice;
            UNTIL WebshopItemLink.NEXT = 0;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            CASE Shop."Retail Price Typ" OF
              Shop."Base Price Typ"::"Sales Price": BEGIN
                RetailPrice := Item."Unit Price";
                IF Shop."Prices Including VAT" AND NOT "Price Includes VAT" THEN
                  IF VATPostingSetup.GET(Shop."VAT Bus. Posting Group","VAT Prod. Posting Group") THEN
                    RetailPrice := RetailPrice / 100 * (100 + VATPostingSetup."VAT %");
                IF NOT Shop."Prices Including VAT" AND "Price Includes VAT" THEN
                  IF VATPostingSetup.GET(Shop."VAT Bus. Posting Group","VAT Prod. Posting Group") THEN
                    RetailPrice := RetailPrice / (100 + VATPostingSetup."VAT %") * 100;
              END;
              Shop."Base Price Typ"::"Customer Price Group": BEGIN
                AllCustPrice := WebshopGetPrice(TRUE,'','',Shop."Prices Including VAT",Shop."VAT Bus. Posting Group");
                RetailPrice := WebshopGetPrice(FALSE,Shop."Cust. Price Group Retail Price",Shop."Cust. Disc. Group Retail Price",
                  Shop."Prices Including VAT",Shop."VAT Bus. Posting Group");
                IF ((RetailPrice > AllCustPrice) AND (AllCustPrice <> 0)) OR (RetailPrice = 0) THEN
                  RetailPrice := AllCustPrice;
              END;
              Shop."Base Price Typ"::"All Customer Price": RetailPrice := WebshopGetPrice(TRUE,'','',
                Shop."Prices Including VAT",Shop."VAT Bus. Posting Group");
            END;
          END;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE WebshopBasePrice@1000000003() BasePrice : Decimal;
    VAR
      Item@1000000000 : Record 27;
      WebshopItem@1000000002 : Record 5447304;
      "Webshop Item Link"@1000000003 : Record 5447308;
      SalesPrice@1000000001 : Record 7002;
      AllowLineDisc@1000000004 : Boolean;
      AllCustPrice@1000000005 : Decimal;
      GroupPrice@1000000006 : Decimal;
      VATPostingSetup@1000000007 : Record 325;
    BEGIN
      // Ermittelt Webshop-Basispreis anhand Shop-Einrichtung

      WITH Item DO BEGIN
        Shop.GET("Shop Code");
        IF GET("Item No.") THEN BEGIN
          WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE(Typ,WebshopItemLink.Typ::Variant);
          WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code",Shop.Code);
          WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE("Item No.","No.");
          IF (Shop."Variant Typ" = Shop."Variant Typ"::ShopVariantsParentItem)
          AND (WebshopItemLink.FINDSET) THEN BEGIN
            REPEAT
              WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Shop Code",Shop.Code);
              WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
              WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Item No.",WebshopItemLink."Linked Item No.");
              WebshopItem.SETRANGE(Active,TRUE);
              IF WebshopItem.FINDFIRST THEN
                IF (BasePrice > WebshopItem.WebshopBasePrice) OR (BasePrice = 0) THEN
                  BasePrice := WebshopItem.WebshopBasePrice;
            UNTIL WebshopItemLink.NEXT = 0;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            CASE Shop."Base Price Typ" OF
              Shop."Base Price Typ"::"Sales Price": BEGIN
                BasePrice := Item."Unit Price";
                IF Shop."Prices Including VAT" AND NOT "Price Includes VAT" THEN
                  IF VATPostingSetup.GET(Shop."VAT Bus. Posting Group","VAT Prod. Posting Group") THEN
                    BasePrice := BasePrice / 100 * (100 + VATPostingSetup."VAT %");
                IF NOT Shop."Prices Including VAT" AND "Price Includes VAT" THEN
                  IF VATPostingSetup.GET(Shop."VAT Bus. Posting Group","VAT Prod. Posting Group") THEN
                    BasePrice := BasePrice / (100 + VATPostingSetup."VAT %") * 100;
              END;
              Shop."Base Price Typ"::"Customer Price Group": BEGIN
                AllCustPrice := WebshopGetPrice(TRUE,'','',Shop."Prices Including VAT",Shop."VAT Bus. Posting Group");
                BasePrice := WebshopGetPrice(FALSE,Shop."Cust. Price Group Base Price",Shop."Cust. Disc. Group Base Price",
                  Shop."Prices Including VAT",Shop."VAT Bus. Posting Group");
                IF ((BasePrice > AllCustPrice) AND (AllCustPrice <> 0)) OR (BasePrice = 0) THEN
                  BasePrice := AllCustPrice;
              END;
              Shop."Base Price Typ"::"All Customer Price": BasePrice := WebshopGetPrice(TRUE,'','',
                Shop."Prices Including VAT",Shop."VAT Bus. Posting Group");
            END;
          END;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE WebshopGetPrice@1000000004(AllCustomers@1000000000 : Boolean;CustPriceGroup@1000000001 : Code[10];CustDiscGroup@1000000002 : Code[10];IncVAT@1000000012 : Boolean;VATBusPostingGroup@1000000014 : Code[10]) BestPrice : Decimal;
    VAR
      SalesPrice@1000000003 : Record 7002;
      SalesLineDiscount@1000000004 : Record 7004;
      VATPostingSetup@1000000013 : Record 325;
      AllowLineDisc@1000000005 : Boolean;
      BestPriceIncVAT@1000000011 : Boolean;
      Item@1000000006 : Record 27;
      BestItemLineDiscount@1000000007 : Decimal;
      BestItemDiscGroupLineDiscount@1000000009 : Decimal;
      BestLineDiscount@1000000008 : Decimal;
      TempLineDiscount@1000000010 : Decimal;
    BEGIN
      // Ermittelt besten Preis abz�glich Zeilenrabatt f�r einen Artikel f�r "Alle Debitoren" oder eine bestimmte Debitorengruppe

      Item.GET("Item No.");
      BestPrice := 0;

      SalesPrice.SETRANGE("Item No.",Item."No.");
      IF AllCustomers THEN BEGIN
        SalesPrice.SETRANGE("Sales Type",SalesPrice."Sales Type"::"All Customers");
      END ELSE BEGIN
        SalesPrice.SETRANGE("Sales Type",SalesPrice."Sales Type"::"Customer Price Group");
        SalesPrice.SETRANGE("Sales Code",CustPriceGroup);
      END;
      SalesPrice.SETFILTER("Unit of Measure Code",'%1|%2',"Unit of Measure Code",'');
      SalesPrice.SETFILTER("Starting Date",'<=%1|%2',TODAY,0D);
      SalesPrice.SETFILTER("Ending Date",'>=%1|%2',TODAY,0D);
      SalesPrice.SETFILTER("Currency Code",'%1','');
      SalesPrice.SETRANGE("Minimum Quantity",0,1);
      IF SalesPrice.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
        BestPrice := 0;
        REPEAT
          IF (BestPrice > SalesPrice."Unit Price") OR (BestPrice = 0) THEN BEGIN
            BestPrice := SalesPrice."Unit Price";
            AllowLineDisc := SalesPrice."Allow Line Disc.";
            BestPriceIncVAT := SalesPrice."Price Includes VAT";
          END;
        UNTIL SalesPrice.NEXT = 0;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        IF AllCustomers THEN BEGIN
          BestPrice := Item."Unit Price";
          AllowLineDisc := TRUE;
          BestPriceIncVAT := Item."Price Includes VAT";
        END;
      END;

      IF (BestPrice <> 0) AND (AllowLineDisc) THEN BEGIN
        BestLineDiscount := WebshopLineDiscount(TRUE,FALSE,'');
        TempLineDiscount := WebshopLineDiscount(TRUE,TRUE,'');
        IF TempLineDiscount > BestLineDiscount THEN
          BestLineDiscount := TempLineDiscount;
        IF CustDiscGroup <> '' THEN BEGIN
          TempLineDiscount := WebshopLineDiscount(FALSE,FALSE,CustDiscGroup);
          IF TempLineDiscount > BestLineDiscount THEN
            BestLineDiscount := TempLineDiscount;
          TempLineDiscount := WebshopLineDiscount(FALSE,TRUE,CustDiscGroup);
          IF TempLineDiscount > BestLineDiscount THEN
            BestLineDiscount := TempLineDiscount;
        END;
        IF BestLineDiscount <> 0 THEN BEGIN
          BestPrice := BestPrice / 100 * (100-BestLineDiscount);
        END;
      END;

      IF IncVAT AND NOT BestPriceIncVAT THEN
        IF VATPostingSetup.GET(VATBusPostingGroup,Item."VAT Prod. Posting Group") THEN
          BestPrice := BestPrice / 100 * (100 + VATPostingSetup."VAT %");
      IF NOT IncVAT AND BestPriceIncVAT THEN
        IF VATPostingSetup.GET(VATBusPostingGroup,Item."VAT Prod. Posting Group") THEN
          BestPrice := BestPrice / (100 + VATPostingSetup."VAT %") * 100;
    END;

    PROCEDURE WebshopLineDiscount@1000000008(AllCustomers@1000000000 : Boolean;ItemDiscGroup@1000000001 : Boolean;CustDiscGroup@1000000002 : Code[10]) BestLineDiscount : Decimal;
    VAR
      SalesLineDiscount@1000000004 : Record 7004;
      AllowLineDisc@1000000005 : Boolean;
      Item@1000000006 : Record 27;
    BEGIN
      // Ermittelt besten Zeilenrabatt f�r einen Artikel oder eine Artikelrabattgruppe f�r "Alle Debitoren" oder Debitorenrabattgruppe

      Item.GET("Item No.");
      BestLineDiscount := 0;
      IF ItemDiscGroup THEN BEGIN
        SalesLineDiscount.SETRANGE(Type,SalesLineDiscount.Type::"Item Disc. Group");
        SalesLineDiscount.SETRANGE(Code,Item."Item Disc. Group");
      END ELSE BEGIN
        SalesLineDiscount.SETRANGE(Type,SalesLineDiscount.Type::Item);
        SalesLineDiscount.SETRANGE(Code,Item."No.");
      END;
      IF AllCustomers THEN BEGIN
        SalesLineDiscount.SETRANGE("Sales Type",SalesLineDiscount."Sales Type"::"All Customers");
      END ELSE BEGIN
        SalesLineDiscount.SETRANGE("Sales Type",SalesLineDiscount."Sales Type"::"Customer Disc. Group");
        SalesLineDiscount.SETRANGE("Sales Code",CustDiscGroup);
      END;
      SalesLineDiscount.SETFILTER("Unit of Measure Code",'%1|%2',"Unit of Measure Code",'');
      SalesLineDiscount.SETFILTER("Starting Date",'<=%1|%2',TODAY,0D);
      SalesLineDiscount.SETFILTER("Ending Date",'>=%1|%2',TODAY,0D);
      SalesLineDiscount.SETFILTER("Currency Code",'%1','');
      SalesLineDiscount.SETRANGE("Minimum Quantity",0,1);
      IF SalesLineDiscount.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
        IF BestLineDiscount < SalesLineDiscount."Line Discount %" THEN
          BestLineDiscount := SalesLineDiscount."Line Discount %";
      UNTIL SalesLineDiscount.NEXT = 0;
    END;

    PROCEDURE WebshopOrderRanking@1000000005() OrderRanking : Decimal;
    VAR
      Item@1000000000 : Record 27;
      Item2@1000000001 : Record 27;
      WebshopItemLink@1000000002 : Record 5447308;
    BEGIN
      WITH Item DO BEGIN
        IF GET("Item No.") THEN BEGIN
          OrderRanking := 0;
          WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE("Item No.","No.");
          WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code",Rec."Shop Code");
          WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE(Typ,WebshopItemLink.Typ::Variant);
          IF WebshopItemLink.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
            REPEAT
              IF Item2.GET(WebshopItemLink."Linked Item No.") THEN BEGIN
                Item2.SETRANGE("Date Filter",CALCDATE('<CD-1Y>'),TODAY);
                Item2.CALCFIELDS("Sales (Qty.)");
                OrderRanking += Item2."Sales (Qty.)";
              END;
            UNTIL WebshopItemLink.NEXT = 0;
          END;
          SETRANGE("Date Filter",CALCDATE('<CD-1Y>'),TODAY);
          CALCFIELDS("Sales (Qty.)");
          OrderRanking += "Sales (Qty.)";
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE NoOfActiveVariants@1000000006() NoOfVariant : Integer;
    VAR
      WebshopItemLink@1000000000 : Record 5447308;
      WebshopItem@1000000001 : Record 5447304;
    BEGIN
      NoOfVariant := 0;
      WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
      WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE(Typ,WebshopItemLink.Typ::Variant);
      IF WebshopItemLink.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
        WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Item No.",WebshopItemLink."Item No.");
        WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
        WebshopItem.SETRANGE(Active,TRUE);
        WebshopItem.SETFILTER("Validity from",'<=%1|%2',TODAY,0D);
        WebshopItem.SETFILTER("Validity to",'>=%1|%2',TODAY,0D);
        IF WebshopItem.FINDFIRST THEN
          NoOfVariant += 1;
      UNTIL WebshopItemLink.NEXT = 0;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetItemCanonicalNameUrlEncoded@1000000002() CanonicalNameURL : Text[1024];
    VAR
      CanonicalName@1000000002 : Text[255];
      WebshopManagement@1000000001 : Codeunit 5447300;
    BEGIN
      CanonicalName := Description;
      IF "Variant Description" <> '' THEN
        CanonicalName += ' ' + WebshopManagement.StrReplace("Variant Description",Description,'');
      CanonicalNameURL := WebshopManagement.URLEncodeReadable(CanonicalName);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetCanonicalItemCategoryURL@1000000007() CanonicalCategoryURL : Text[1024];
    VAR
      WebshopManagement@1000000000 : Codeunit 5447300;
      WebshopItemCategory@1000000001 : Record 5447305;
      WebshopShop@1000000002 : Record 5447301;
      WebshopLanguage@1000000003 : Record 5447302;
      CategoryShopCode@1000000004 : Code[30];
      CategoryLanguageCode@1000000005 : Code[10];
      WebshopItem@1000000006 : Record 5447304;
    BEGIN
      WITH WebshopItem DO BEGIN
        GET(Rec."Shop Code",Rec."Language Code",Rec."Item No.");
        CALCFIELDS("Is Variant");
        CALCFIELDS("Parent Item No.");

        WebshopShop.GET("Shop Code");
        WebshopLanguage.GET("Shop Code","Language Code");

        CategoryLanguageCode := WebshopLanguage.Code;

        IF WebshopShop."Use Categorys from Shop Code" <> '' THEN
          CategoryShopCode := WebshopShop."Use Categorys from Shop Code"
        ELSE
          CategoryShopCode := "Shop Code";

        IF "Is Variant" AND ("Parent Item No." <> '') THEN BEGIN
          GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Parent Item No.");
        END;

        IF "Main Category Line No." <> 0 THEN BEGIN
          IF WebshopItemCategory.GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Item No.",CategoryShopCode,CategoryLanguageCode,
      "Main Category Line No.") THEN
            CanonicalCategoryURL := WebshopItemCategory.GetItemCategoryPath;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          WebshopItemCategory.SETRANGE("Item Shop Code","Shop Code");
          WebshopItemCategory.SETRANGE("Item Language Code","Language Code");
          WebshopItemCategory.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
          WebshopItemCategory.SETRANGE("Category Shop Code",CategoryShopCode);
          WebshopItemCategory.SETRANGE("Category Language Code",CategoryLanguageCode);
          IF WebshopItemCategory.FINDFIRST THEN
            CanonicalCategoryURL := WebshopItemCategory.GetItemCategoryPath
          ELSE
            CanonicalCategoryURL := '/' + GetItemCanonicalNameUrlEncoded + '/';
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE WebshopCustomizePrice@1000000009() CustomizePrice : Decimal;
    VAR
      Item@1000000000 : Record 27;
      WebshopItem@1000000002 : Record 5447304;
      "Webshop Item Link"@1000000003 : Record 5447308;
      SalesPrice@1000000001 : Record 7002;
      AllowLineDisc@1000000004 : Boolean;
      AllCustPrice@1000000005 : Decimal;
      GroupPrice@1000000006 : Decimal;
      VATPostingSetup@1000000007 : Record 325;
    BEGIN
      // Ermittelt Webshop-Individualisierungspreis anhand Shop-Einrichtung

      WITH Item DO BEGIN
        Shop.GET("Shop Code");
        IF GET("Item No.") THEN BEGIN
          WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE(Typ,WebshopItemLink.Typ::Variant);
          WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code",Shop.Code);
          WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE("Item No.","No.");
          IF (Shop."Variant Typ" = Shop."Variant Typ"::ShopVariantsParentItem)
          AND (WebshopItemLink.FINDSET) THEN BEGIN
            REPEAT
              WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Shop Code",Shop.Code);
              WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
              WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Item No.",WebshopItemLink."Linked Item No.");
              WebshopItem.SETRANGE(Active,TRUE);
              IF WebshopItem.FINDFIRST THEN
                IF (CustomizePrice > WebshopItem.WebshopCustomizePrice) OR (CustomizePrice = 0) THEN
                  CustomizePrice := WebshopItem.WebshopCustomizePrice;
            UNTIL WebshopItemLink.NEXT = 0;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            CASE Shop."Customize Price Typ" OF
              Shop."Customize Price Typ"::"Sales Price": BEGIN
                CustomizePrice := Item."Unit Price";
                IF Shop."Prices Including VAT" AND NOT "Price Includes VAT" THEN
                  IF VATPostingSetup.GET(Shop."VAT Bus. Posting Group","VAT Prod. Posting Group") THEN
                    CustomizePrice := CustomizePrice / 100 * (100 + VATPostingSetup."VAT %");
                IF NOT Shop."Prices Including VAT" AND "Price Includes VAT" THEN
                  IF VATPostingSetup.GET(Shop."VAT Bus. Posting Group","VAT Prod. Posting Group") THEN
                    CustomizePrice := CustomizePrice / (100 + VATPostingSetup."VAT %") * 100;
              END;
              Shop."Customize Price Typ"::"Customer Price Group": BEGIN
                AllCustPrice := WebshopGetPrice(TRUE,'','',Shop."Prices Including VAT",Shop."VAT Bus. Posting Group");
                CustomizePrice := WebshopGetPrice(FALSE,Shop."Cust. Price Group Cust. Price",Shop."Cust. Disc. Group Cust. Price",
                  Shop."Prices Including VAT",Shop."VAT Bus. Posting Group");
              END;
              Shop."Customize Price Typ"::"All Customer Price": CustomizePrice := WebshopGetPrice(TRUE,'','',
                Shop."Prices Including VAT",Shop."VAT Bus. Posting Group");
            END;
          END;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- dynamic commerce - dynamic commerce GmbH - www.dc-solution.de ---
    }
    END.
  }
}

    PROCEDURE GetCategoryPath@1000000000() CategoryPath : Text[1024];

    BEGIN
      InWebshopItemDelete := InDelete;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetItemCategoryPath@1000000002() ItemCategoryURL : Text[1024];
    VAR
      Category@1000000000 : Record 5447303;
      WebshopItem@1000000001 : Record 5447304;
      CategoryURL@1000000003 : Text[512];
      ItemURL@1000000002 : Text[512];
    BEGIN
      IF Category.GET("Category Shop Code","Category Language Code","Category Line No.") THEN
        CategoryURL := Category.GetCategoryURL;
      IF WebshopItem.GET("Item Shop Code","Item Language Code","Item No.") THEN
        ItemURL := WebshopItem.GetItemCanonicalNameUrlEncoded + '/';
      ItemCategoryURL := CategoryURL + ItemURL;
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- dynamic commerce - dynamic commerce GmbH - www.dc-solution.de ---
    }
    END.
  }

OBJECT Table 5447308 Webshop Item Link
{
  OBJECT-PROPERTIES
  {
    Date=30.06.17;
    Time=16:00:00;
    Version List=MYSW14.00;
  }
  PROPERTIES
  {
    OnInsert=BEGIN
               // Webshop-Artikel zur Aktualisierung kennzeichnen
               WebshopManagement.SetWebshopItemModified("Item No.",TRUE,FALSE,FALSE);
             END;

    OnModify=BEGIN
               // Webshop-Artikel zur Aktualisierung kennzeichnen
               WebshopManagement.SetWebshopItemModified("Item No.",TRUE,FALSE,FALSE);
             END;

    OnDelete=BEGIN
               // Webshop-Artikel zur Aktualisierung kennzeichnen
               IF NOT DeleteItem THEN
                 WebshopManagement.SetWebshopItemModified("Item No.",TRUE,FALSE,FALSE);

               // Web-Datensatz l�schen
               IF WebshopManagement.ConnectionTest THEN
                 WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(TRUE);
                 WebshopUpdateManagement.DeleteWebshopItemLink(Rec);
                 WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(FALSE);
             END;

    OnRename=BEGIN
               ERROR(Text50000);
             END;

    CaptionML=DEU=Webshop Artikelverkn�pfung;
    LookupFormID=Form5447315;
    DrillDownFormID=Form5447315;
  }
  FIELDS
  {
    { 1   ;   ;Item No.            ;Code20        ;TableRelation=Item.No.;
                                                   CaptionML=[DEU=Artikelnr.;
                                                              ENU=Item No.];
                                                   NotBlank=Yes }
    { 2   ;   ;Typ                 ;Option        ;CaptionML=[DEU=Typ;
                                                              ENU=Type];
                                                   OptionCaptionML=DEU=Variante,Zubeh�rartikel,Ersatzteil,Alternativartikel,Passender Artikel;
                                                   OptionString=Variant,Acc. Item,Spare Part,Alt. Item,Fit. Item }
    { 3   ;   ;Linked Item No.     ;Code20        ;TableRelation=Item.No.;
                                                   OnValidate=BEGIN
                                                                // Artikel darf nicht mit sich selbst verkn�pft werden
                                                                IF "Linked Item No." = "Item No." THEN
                                                                  FIELDERROR("Linked Item No.");

                                                                // Auf Wunsch Web-Shopartikel anlegen falls nicht vorhanden
                                                                Language.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
                                                                IF Language.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
                                                                  WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
                                                                  WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Language Code",Language.Code);
                                                                  WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Item No.","Linked Item No.");
                                                                  IF NOT WebshopItem.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
                                                                    IF CONFIRM(Text50003,TRUE,"Linked Item No.",Language.Code) THEN BEGIN
                                                                      WebshopItem.INIT;
                                                                      WebshopItem.VALIDATE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
                                                                      WebshopItem.VALIDATE("Language Code",Language.Code);
                                                                      WebshopItem.VALIDATE("Item No.","Linked Item No.");
                                                                      WebshopItem.VALIDATE(Active,TRUE);
                                                                      WebshopItem.INSERT(TRUE);
                                                                    END;
                                                                  END;
                                                                UNTIL Language.NEXT = 0;

                                                                // Ein Mutterartikel darf selbst keine Variante sein
                                                                IF (Typ = Typ::Variant) THEN BEGIN
                                                                  WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
                                                                  WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE(Typ,Typ);
                                                                  WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE("Linked Item No.","Item No.");
                                                                  IF WebshopItemLink.FINDFIRST THEN
                                                                    ERROR(Text50002,"Item No.");
                                                                END;

                                                                // Eine Variante darf nicht als Cross-Selling Artikel verkn�pft werden verkn�pft werden
                                                                IF Typ <> Typ::Variant THEN BEGIN
                                                                  WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
                                                                  WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE(Typ,Typ::Variant);
                                                                  WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE("Linked Item No.","Linked Item No.");
                                                                  IF WebshopItemLink.FINDFIRST THEN
                                                                    ERROR(Text50002,"Linked Item No.");
                                                                END;

                                                                // Ein als Variante verkn�pfter Artikel darf selbst keine Varianten haben
                                                                IF Typ = Typ::Variant THEN BEGIN
                                                                  WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
                                                                  WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE(Typ,Typ::Variant);
                                                                  WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE("Item No.","Linked Item No.");
                                                                  IF WebshopItemLink.FINDFIRST THEN
                                                                   ERROR(Text50001,"Linked Item No.");
                                                                END;
                                                              END;

                                                   CaptionML=[DEU=Artikelnr.;
                                                              ENU=Linked Item No.];
                                                   NotBlank=Yes }
    { 4   ;   ;Linked Item Description;Text50     ;FieldClass=FlowField;
                                                   CalcFormula=Lookup(Item.Description WHERE (No.=FIELD(Linked Item No.)));
                                                   CaptionML=[DEU=Beschreibung;
                                                              ENU=Linked Item Description];
                                                   Editable=No }
    { 5   ;   ;Shop Code           ;Code10        ;TableRelation="Webshop Shop".Code;
                                                   CaptionML=[DEU=Shopcode;
                                                              ENU=Shop Code] }
  }
  KEYS
  {
    {    ;Typ,Shop Code,Item No.,Linked Item No.  ;Clustered=Yes }
  }
  FIELDGROUPS
  {
  }
  CODE
  {
    VAR
      WebshopItemLink@1000000007 : Record 5447308;
      WebshopItem@1000000008 : Record 5447304;
      Language@1000000002 : Record 5447302;
      WebshopManagement@1000000000 : Codeunit 5447300;
      Text50000@1000000001 : TextConst 'DEU=Datensatz darf nicht umbenannt werden';
      WebshopUpdateManagement@1000000006 : Codeunit 5447301;
      Text50001@1000000003 : TextConst 'DEU=Artikel %1 hat selbst Varianten und darf deshalb nicht verkn�pft werden';
      Text50002@1000000004 : TextConst 'DEU=Artikel %1 ist eine Variante und darf deshalb nicht verkn�pft werden';
      Text50003@1000000005 : TextConst 'DEU=Artikel %1 ist kein Webshop-Artikel f�r Sprache %2, soll ein Webshop-Artikel angelegt und aktiv geschaltet werden?';
      DeleteItem@1000000009 : Boolean;

    PROCEDURE SetDeleteItem@1000000000(LocalDeleteItem@1000000000 : Boolean);

  {

    PROCEDURE GetOpenWebshopOrders@1000000000() : Integer;
    VAR
      SalesHeader@1000000000 : Record 36;
    BEGIN
      SalesHeader.SETRANGE("Document Type",SalesHeader."Document Type"::Order);
      SalesHeader.SETRANGE("Document Date","Date Filter");
      SalesHeader.SETFILTER("Webshop Shop Code",'<>%1','');
      SalesHeader.SETRANGE(Status,SalesHeader.Status::Open);
      EXIT(SalesHeader.COUNT);
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- dynamic commerce - dynamic commerce GmbH - www.dc-solution.de ---
    }
    END.
  }
}

    PROCEDURE SendRequest@1000000015(Action@1000000001 : Text[100];Data@1000000002 : Text[1024];ClearBuffer@1000000019 : Boolean;SendNow@1000000000 : Boolean) Response : Boolean;
    VAR
      Request@1000000003 : Text[1024];
      Error200@1000000004 : TextConst 'DEU=Verbindung zum Webshop konnte nicht aufgebaut werden\Aktion: %1';
      Error300@1000000005 : TextConst 'DEU=Keine Antwort vom Webshop erhalten\Aktion: %1';

      LogFile@1000000009 : File;
      Error400@1000000013 : TextConst 'DEU=Fehlerhafte Antwort vom Webshop erhalten\Aktion: %1\Antwort: %2';
      Log001@1000000010 : TextConst 'DEU=### SendRequest: %1 - %2 ###';
      Log002@1000000011 : TextConst;
      Log003@1000000012 : TextConst;
      LogResponse@1000000016 : Text[1024];
      i@1000000015 : Integer;
      ResponseText@1000000017 : Text[1024];
      HTTPRequest@1000 : DotNet "'Microsoft.MSXML, Version=8.0.0.0, Culture=neutral, PublicKeyToken=b03f5f7f11d50a3a'.MSXML.XMLHTTPRequestClass";
      Counter1@1001 : Integer;
      Counter2@1002 : Integer;
      ReqReadyState@1003 : Integer;
      SleepDuration@1004 : Duration;
      SleepUntilDateTime@1005 : DateTime;
      DataSent@1006 : Boolean;
      ResponseAsText@1007 : Text[1024];
      LogResponseText@1008 : Text[1024];
      DoRequest@1000000007 : Boolean;
      DataChanged@1000000014 : Boolean;
      NewReqLength@1000000018 : Integer;
      BufferHasContent@1000000006 : Boolean;
      CurrData@1000000020 : Text[1024];
      ReqData@1000000021 : Text[1024];
      NewBufferData@1000000022 : Text[1024];
      BLOBBigText@1009 : BigText;
      StreamReader@1010 : DotNet "'mscorlib, Version=4.0.0.0, Culture=neutral, PublicKeyToken=b77a5c561934e089'.System.IO.StreamReader";
      BLOBTextLength@1011 : Integer;
      ReqState@1000000023 : Integer;
      SendComplete@1000000024 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      //Control function for webrequest handling with buffer

      //PARAMETERS
      //Action:       Current webrequest action
      //Data:         Current webrequest data
      //ClearBuffer:  Flush buffer to webserver
      //SendNow:      Send current request instead of appending to Buffer

      //When the stringlength of the request-data in the buffer exceeds a given limit
      //it is flushed to the webserver irrespective of whether the Parameter 'ClearBuffer' is set.

      //INTEGRATION
      //In Update-CUs, add "WebshopUpdateBuffer" as global variable
      //Replace "WebshopManagement.SendRequest(ACTION,Data)" with "WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest(ACTION,Data,ClearBuffer,SendNow);
      //BE CAREFUL WITH DELETE-STATEMENTS - always pass "SendNow" as TRUE when calling delete or any webfunction from a button-press
      //Replace Get-Requests - or don't... data-fetches will never go to buffer, but can be handled from this table
      //Modify Navconnect/-Functions to handle URL-encoded,stringified JSON-Object with further requests to fill POST-Superglobal Array
      //Done

      z += 1;
      //GET Setup, Reset Variables
      WebshopSetup.GET;
      ReqData := '';
      CurrData := '';
      NewData := '';
      CLEAR(BLOBBigText);
      CLEAR(BLOBText);
      CLEAR(ReqInstream);
      CLEAR(StreamReader);
      CLEAR(ReqOutstream);
      CLEAR(BufferHasContent);
      //Initialize & Insert if not exists
      IF NOT GET THEN BEGIN
        INIT;
        INSERT;
      END;

      //Evaluate buffer content
      CALCFIELDS(SendRequestBuffer);
      IF SendRequestBuffer.HASVALUE THEN BEGIN
        SendRequestBuffer.CREATEINSTREAM(ReqInstream);
        StreamReader := StreamReader.StreamReader(ReqInstream, TRUE);
        BLOBText := StreamReader.ReadToEnd();
        BLOBTextLength := STRLEN(BLOBText);
        IF BLOBTextLength>0 THEN
          BufferHasContent := TRUE;
      END;

      //Exit true if nothing to do
      IF (STRLEN(Action+Data)=0) AND ClearBuffer AND (NOT BufferHasContent) THEN
        EXIT(TRUE);

      //Prepare request wrapper if there is anything to send
      //'%5B' starts URL-encoded JSON-Object-Wrapper for following requests
      IF SendNow OR ClearBuffer OR (BufferHasContent AND (BLOBTextLength>150000)) THEN
        ReqData += 'json=true&pass='+WebshopSetup."HTTP Password"+'&wrapperdata='+'%5B';

      //Create URL-encoded, stringified JSON-array for current request if exists
      IF STRLEN(Action+Data)>1 THEN
        CurrData := '%7B%22reqaction%22%3A%22'+Action+'%22%2C%22reqdata%22%3A%22'+WebshopManagement.EncodeURIComponent(Data)+'%22%7D';

      //If buffer is to be flushed, append buffer-string to ReqData
      IF (ClearBuffer AND BufferHasContent) OR (BufferHasContent AND (BLOBTextLength>150000)) THEN BEGIN
        ReqData += BLOBText;
        //Append URL-encoded Comma if current request must be appended as well
        IF SendNow AND (STRLEN(Action+Data)>1) THEN
          ReqData += '%2C';
      END;

      //Concatenate (possibly empty) string-representation of current request to ReqData if and only if parameter 'SendNow' isset
      IF SendNow THEN
        ReqData := ReqData+CurrData;

      //Close wrapper if there is anything to send
      IF SendNow OR ClearBuffer OR (BufferHasContent AND (BLOBTextLength>150000)) THEN
        ReqData += '%5D';

      //If combined request from buffer and current request is not empty, set 'DoRequest' Flag
      IF STRLEN(ReqData)>0 THEN
        DoRequest := TRUE;

      //If data is to be written to the buffer, construct the string for its new content
      IF NOT ((ClearBuffer AND BufferHasContent) OR (BufferHasContent AND (BLOBTextLength>150000))) THEN BEGIN
        IF (BLOBTextLength>0) AND (STRLEN(CurrData)>0) THEN
          NewData := BLOBText+'%2C'+CurrData
        ELSE
          NewData := CurrData
      END;

      //There is something to send
      IF DoRequest THEN BEGIN
        NewReqLength := STRLEN(ReqData);
        Request := WebshopSetup."HTTP Adress Connector" + 'navconnect.php?legacy=1&action=wrapper';

        //Write to Logfile if setup-option is true
        IF WebshopSetup."Log Active" THEN BEGIN
          LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
          LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
          IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
            LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Log File")
          ELSE
            LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Log File");
          LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
          LogFile.WRITE(STRSUBSTNO(Log001,Action,CURRENTDATETIME));
          IF ReqData <> '' THEN
            LogFile.WRITE(COPYSTR(ReqData,1,1020));
          LogFile.CLOSE;
        END;

        //Open Asynchronous HTTP-Webrequest over .NET MSXML
        HTTPRequest := HTTPRequest.XMLHTTPRequestClass;
        HTTPRequest.open('POST',Request,TRUE,'','');
        HTTPRequest.setRequestHeader('Content-Type','application/x-www-form-urlencoded');
        HTTPRequest.ToString();
        HTTPRequest.send(ReqData);

        //Check ReadyState of XMLHTTPRequest periodically with given waiting time
        //for a set number of iterations or until the ReadyState is read as completed successfully
        Counter1 := 0;
        SleepDuration := 30;
        WHILE (HTTPRequest.readyState<2) AND (Counter1<2001) DO BEGIN
          IF (Counter1=2000) AND (HTTPRequest.readyState<2) THEN BEGIN
            IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
              ERROR('Could not send Data. Request: '+Request+'; Data: '+ReqData+' Ready State'+FORMAT(HTTPRequest.readyState))
            ELSE BEGIN
              //Otherwise try to log error, abort and throw error if logging is unsuccessful
              IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Log File")>0 THEN BEGIN
                LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
                LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
                IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN BEGIN
                 IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                  ERROR('Could not create logfile at specified location');
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                    ERROR('Could not open existing logfile');
                END;
                LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
                LogFile.WRITE(FORMAT(CURRENTDATETIME)+' - Could not send Data. Request: '+Request+'; Data: '+ReqData+' Ready State'+
      FORMAT(HTTPRequest.readyState));
                LogFile.CLOSE;
                //If user-interface exists and setup-option is set, message errors to user
                IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                  MESSAGE(FORMAT(CURRENTDATETIME)+' - Could not send Data. Request: '+Request+'; Data: '+ReqData+' Ready State'+FORMAT(
      HTTPRequest.readyState));
                EXIT(TRUE);
              END ELSE
                ERROR('No Logfile specified');
            END;
          END;

          IF (HTTPRequest.readyState=0) THEN BEGIN
            HTTPRequest.abort();
            HTTPRequest.open('POST',Request,TRUE,'','');
            HTTPRequest.setRequestHeader('Content-Type','application/x-www-form-urlencoded');
            HTTPRequest.ToString();
            HTTPRequest.send(ReqData);
          END;
          SLEEP(SleepDuration);
        END;

        IF (HTTPRequest.readyState>1) THEN
          SendComplete := TRUE;


        WHILE (HTTPRequest.readyState<>4) AND (Counter1<1201) DO BEGIN
          ReqReadyState := HTTPRequest.readyState;
          Counter2 := 0;

          //Abort if not successfully finished after max. no of iterations
          IF (Counter1=1200) AND (ReqReadyState<>4) THEN BEGIN
            //Throw error if ignore-option in setup is not true
            IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
              ERROR('Could not send Data. Request: '+Request+'; Data: '+ReqData+' Ready State'+FORMAT(HTTPRequest.readyState))
            ELSE BEGIN
              //Otherwise try to log error, abort and throw error if logging is unsuccessful
              IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Log File")>0 THEN BEGIN
                LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
                LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
                IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN BEGIN
                 IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                  ERROR('Could not create logfile at specified location');
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                    ERROR('Could not open existing logfile');
                END;
                LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
                LogFile.WRITE(FORMAT(CURRENTDATETIME)+' - Could not send Data. Request: '+Request+'; Data: '+ReqData+' Ready State'+
      FORMAT(HTTPRequest.readyState));
                LogFile.CLOSE;
                //If user-interface exists and setup-option is set, message errors to user
                IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                  MESSAGE(FORMAT(CURRENTDATETIME)+' - Could not send Data. Request: '+Request+'; Data: '+ReqData+' Ready State'+FORMAT(
      HTTPRequest.readyState));
                EXIT(TRUE);
              END ELSE
                ERROR('No Logfile specified');
            END;
          END;

          //If the request failed in a way result in a reset, wait and re-send
          IF (ReqReadyState=0) THEN BEGIN
            SleepUntilDateTime := CURRENTDATETIME + SleepDuration;
            WHILE (CURRENTDATETIME <= SleepUntilDateTime) DO BEGIN
              Counter2 += 1;
            END;
            SleepUntilDateTime := 0DT;
              HTTPRequest.open('POST',Request,TRUE,'','');
            HTTPRequest.setRequestHeader('Content-Type','application/x-www-form-urlencoded');
            HTTPRequest.ToString();
            HTTPRequest.send(ReqData);
            ReqReadyState := HTTPRequest.readyState;
          END;

          //Wait until one interval has passed (or request has completed succesfully), then increment counter and continue with loop
          SleepUntilDateTime := CURRENTDATETIME + SleepDuration;
          WHILE (CURRENTDATETIME <= SleepUntilDateTime) AND (ReqReadyState<>4) DO BEGIN
            Counter2 += 1;
          END;
          SleepUntilDateTime := 0DT;
          Counter1 += 1;
        END;

        //Loop has finished without exiting or throwing an error
        //Evaluate HTTP-Status Code of Response
        //If response is not "success" throw or log errors and exit true, output as message if desired
        IF (HTTPRequest.status <> 200) THEN BEGIN
          IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN BEGIN
            ERROR(FORMAT(CURRENTDATETIME)+' - Serverside Error Response. Request: '+Request+'; Data: '
            +ReqData+' Response-Code: '+FORMAT(HTTPRequest.status)+'; Response-Text: '+HTTPRequest.statusText);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Log File")>0 THEN BEGIN
              LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
              LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
              IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN BEGIN
               IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                ERROR('Could not create logfile at specified location');
              END ELSE BEGIN
                IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                  ERROR('Could not open existing logfile');
              END;
              LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
              LogFile.WRITE(FORMAT(CURRENTDATETIME)+' - Serverside Error Response. Request: '+Request+'; Data: '
              +ReqData+' Response-Code: '+FORMAT(HTTPRequest.status)+'; Response-Text: '+HTTPRequest.statusText);
              LogFile.CLOSE;
              IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                MESSAGE(FORMAT(CURRENTDATETIME)+' - Serverside Error Response. Request: '+Request+'; Data: '
                +ReqData+' Response-Code: '+FORMAT(HTTPRequest.status)+'; Response-Text: '+HTTPRequest.statusText);
              EXIT(TRUE);
            END ELSE
              ERROR('No Logfile specified');
          END;
        END;
        ReqState := HTTPRequest.status;
        //Server successfully responded to request
        //Get responsetext and log if required
        ResponseAsText := HTTPRequest.responseText;
        IF WebshopSetup."Log Active" THEN BEGIN
          LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
          LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
          IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
            LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Log File")
          ELSE
            LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Log File");
          LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
          i := 1;
          REPEAT
            LogResponseText := COPYSTR(ResponseAsText,i,1020);
            LogFile.WRITE(LogResponseText);
            i += 1020;
          UNTIL i > STRLEN(ResponseAsText);

          LogFile.WRITE('');
          LogFile.CLOSE;
        END;

        //If script-response is "ok" then set flag "response"
        IF ResponseAsText = '100' THEN BEGIN
          Response := TRUE;
          //If request included a buffer-flush, clear the buffer-content variable, set 'data changed' flag and 'last reset' datetime
          IF (ClearBuffer AND BufferHasContent) OR (BufferHasContent AND (BLOBTextLength>150000)) THEN BEGIN
            CLEAR(SendRequestBuffer);
            DataChanged := TRUE;
            "Last Reset" := CURRENTDATETIME;
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          //If Response is not "ok" but not empty, throw error or catch,log and exit true, output as message if desired
          IF ResponseText <> '' THEN BEGIN
            IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
              ERROR(Error400,Action,ResponseText)
            ELSE BEGIN
              IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Log File")>0 THEN BEGIN
                LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
                LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
                IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN BEGIN
                 IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                  ERROR('Could not create logfile at specified location');
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                    ERROR('Could not open existing logfile');
                END;
                LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
                LogFile.WRITE(STRSUBSTNO(Error400,Action,ResponseText));
                LogFile.CLOSE;
                IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                  MESSAGE(STRSUBSTNO(Error400,Action,ResponseText));
                Response := TRUE;
              END ELSE
                ERROR('No Logfile specified');
            END;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            //If request empty, throw error or catch, log and exit true, output as message if desired
            IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
              ERROR(Error300,Action)
            ELSE BEGIN
              IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Log File")>0 THEN BEGIN
                LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
                LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
                IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN BEGIN
                 IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                  ERROR('Could not create logfile at specified location');
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                    ERROR('Could not open existing logfile');
                END;
                LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
                LogFile.WRITE(STRSUBSTNO(Error300,Action));
                LogFile.CLOSE;
                IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                  MESSAGE(STRSUBSTNO(Error300,Action));
                Response := TRUE;
              END ELSE
                ERROR('No Logfile specified');
            END;
          END;
        END;
      END;

      //If there is content for the buffer, stream new data into blob, set flag
      IF (STRLEN(NewData) > 0) THEN BEGIN
        BLOBBigText.ADDTEXT(NewData);
        SendRequestBuffer.CREATEOUTSTREAM(ReqOutstream);
        BLOBBigText.WRITE(ReqOutstream);
        DataChanged := TRUE;
      END;

      //If record has changed, set modified (and reset) date - modify true
      IF DataChanged THEN BEGIN
        "Last Modified" := CURRENTDATETIME;
        IF "Last Reset" > 0DT THEN
          "Last Reset" := "Last Modified";
        MODIFY(TRUE);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetRequest@2(Action@1000000001 : Text[100];Data@1000000002 : Text[1024]) Response : Text[1024];
    VAR
      Request@1000000003 : Text[1024];
      Error200@1000000004 : TextConst 'DEU=Verbindung zum Webshop konnte nicht aufgebaut werden\Aktion: %1';
      Error300@1000000005 : TextConst 'DEU=Keine Antwort vom Webshop erhalten\Aktion: %1';
      BigTextResponse@1000000008 : Text[1024];
      LogFile@1000000009 : File;
      Error400@1000000013 : TextConst 'DEU=Fehlerhafte Antwort vom Webshop erhalten\Aktion: %1\Antwort: %2';
      Log001@1000000010 : TextConst 'DEU=### SendRequest: %1 - %2 ###';
      Log002@1000000011 : TextConst 'DEU="--> "';
      Log003@1000000012 : TextConst 'DEU="<-- "';
      LogResponse@1000000016 : Text[1024];
      i@1000000015 : Integer;
      ResponseText@1000000017 : Text[1024];

      Counter2@1002 : Integer;
      ReqReadyState@1003 : Integer;
      SleepDuration@1004 : Duration;
      SleepUntilDateTime@1005 : DateTime;

      ResponseAsText@1007 : Text[1024];
      LogResponseText@1008 : Text[1024];
      ReqLength@1009 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      // Datensatz senden (Antwort /TRUE/FALSE)

      WebshopSetup.GET;
      Request := WebshopSetup."HTTP Adress Connector" + 'navconnect.php?legacy=1&action=' + Action;
      IF WebshopSetup."Log Active" THEN BEGIN
        LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
        LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
        IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
          LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Log File")
        ELSE
          LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Log File");
        LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
        LogFile.WRITE(STRSUBSTNO(Log001,Action,CURRENTDATETIME));
        IF Data <> '' THEN
          LogFile.WRITE(COPYSTR(Data,1,1020));
        LogFile.CLOSE;
      END;
      Data := Data + 'pass=' + WebshopSetup."HTTP Password";
      //CREATE(HTTPRequest);
      //ResponseText := SendRequestHelp(Request,Data);
      HTTPRequest := HTTPRequest.XMLHTTPRequestClass;

      HTTPRequest.open('POST',Request,TRUE,'','');
      HTTPRequest.setRequestHeader('Content-Type','application/x-www-form-urlencoded');
      HTTPRequest.ToString();
      HTTPRequest.send(Data);

      Counter1 := 0;
      SleepDuration := 25;   //25 Millisekunden Wartezeit
      WHILE (HTTPRequest.readyState<>4) AND (Counter1<121) DO BEGIN
        ReqReadyState := HTTPRequest.readyState;
        Counter2 := 0;
        IF (Counter1=120) AND (ReqReadyState<>4) THEN BEGIN
          IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
            ERROR('Could not send Data. Request: '+Request+'; Data: '+Data+' Ready State'+FORMAT(HTTPRequest.readyState))
          ELSE BEGIN
            IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Log File")>0 THEN BEGIN
              LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
              LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
              IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN BEGIN
               IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                ERROR('Could not create logfile at specified location');
              END ELSE BEGIN
                IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                  ERROR('Could not open existing logfile');
              END;
              LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
              LogFile.WRITE(FORMAT(CURRENTDATETIME)+' - Could not send Data. Request: '+Request+'; Data: '+Data+' Ready State'+FORMAT(
      HTTPRequest.readyState));
              LogFile.CLOSE;
              IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                MESSAGE(FORMAT(CURRENTDATETIME)+' - Could not send Data. Request: '+Request+'; Data: '+Data+' Ready State'+FORMAT(
      HTTPRequest.readyState));
            END ELSE
              ERROR('No Logfile specified');
          END;
        END;
        IF (ReqReadyState=0) THEN BEGIN
          SleepUntilDateTime := CURRENTDATETIME + SleepDuration;
          WHILE (CURRENTDATETIME <= SleepUntilDateTime) AND (ReqReadyState<>4) DO BEGIN
            Counter2 += 1;
          END;
          SleepUntilDateTime := 0DT;
            HTTPRequest.open('POST',Request,TRUE,'','');
          HTTPRequest.setRequestHeader('Content-Type','application/x-www-form-urlencoded');
          HTTPRequest.ToString();
          HTTPRequest.send(Data);
          ReqReadyState := HTTPRequest.readyState;
        END;
        SleepUntilDateTime := CURRENTDATETIME + SleepDuration;
        WHILE (CURRENTDATETIME <= SleepUntilDateTime) AND (ReqReadyState<>4) DO BEGIN
          Counter2 += 1;
        END;
        SleepUntilDateTime := 0DT;
        Counter1 += 1;
      END;

      //Vollst�ndige Antwort
      IF (HTTPRequest.status <> 200) THEN BEGIN
        IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN BEGIN
          ERROR(FORMAT(CURRENTDATETIME)+' - Serverside Error Response. Request: '+Request+'; Data: '
          +Data+' Response-Code: '+FORMAT(HTTPRequest.status)+'; Response-Text: '+HTTPRequest.statusText);
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Log File")>0 THEN BEGIN
            LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
            LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
            IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN BEGIN
             IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
              ERROR('Could not create logfile at specified location');
            END ELSE BEGIN
              IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                ERROR('Could not open existing logfile');
            END;
            LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
            LogFile.WRITE(FORMAT(CURRENTDATETIME)+' - Serverside Error Response. Request: '+Request+'; Data: '
            +Data+' Response-Code: '+FORMAT(HTTPRequest.status)+'; Response-Text: '+HTTPRequest.statusText);
            LogFile.CLOSE;
            IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
              MESSAGE(FORMAT(CURRENTDATETIME)+' - Serverside Error Response. Request: '+Request+'; Data: '
              +Data+' Response-Code: '+FORMAT(HTTPRequest.status)+'; Response-Text: '+HTTPRequest.statusText);
          END ELSE
            ERROR('No Logfile specified');
        END;
      END;


      ResponseAsText := HTTPRequest.responseText;
        IF WebshopSetup."Log Active" THEN BEGIN
          LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
          LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
          IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
            LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Log File")
          ELSE
            LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Log File");
          LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
          i := 1;
          REPEAT
            LogResponseText := COPYSTR(ResponseAsText,i,1020);
            LogFile.WRITE(LogResponseText);
            i += 1020;
          UNTIL i > STRLEN(ResponseAsText);

          LogFile.WRITE('');
          LogFile.CLOSE;
        END;

        Response := ResponseAsText;
    END;

    BEGIN
    END.
  }

  {
    OnModify=VAR
               WebshopItemComment@1000000000 : Record 5447317;

             END;

    CaptionML=[DEU=Webshop Artikelbewertungen;
               ENU=Webshop Item Ratings];

    { 1   ;   ;Item No.            ;Code20        ;TableRelation=Item.No.;

OBJECT Table 5447321 Webshop Attribute Link
{
  OBJECT-PROPERTIES
  {
    Date=30.06.17;
    Time=16:00:00;
    Version List=MYSW14.00;
  }
  PROPERTIES
  {
    OnInsert=BEGIN
               // Reihenfolge beim Einf�gen setzten

               CASE Type OF
                 Type::Item: BEGIN
                   TempAttributeLink.SETCURRENTKEY(Type,"No.",Sorting);
                   TempAttributeLink.SETRANGE(Type,Type::Item);
                   TempAttributeLink.SETRANGE("No.","No.");
                   WebshopManagement.SetWebshopItemModified("No.",TRUE,FALSE,FALSE);
                 END;
                 Type::Category: BEGIN
                   TempAttributeLink.SETCURRENTKEY(Type,"Shop Code","Language Code","Category Line No.",Sorting);
                   TempAttributeLink.SETRANGE(Type,Type::Category);
                   TempAttributeLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
                   TempAttributeLink.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
                   TempAttributeLink.SETRANGE("Category Line No.","Category Line No.");
                   WebshopManagement.SetWebshopCategoryModified("Shop Code","Language Code","Category Line No.");
                 END;
                 Type::Shop: BEGIN
                   TempAttributeLink.SETCURRENTKEY(Type,"Shop Code",Sorting);
                   TempAttributeLink.SETRANGE(Type,Type::Shop);
                   TempAttributeLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
                 END;
                 Type::Template: BEGIN
                   TempAttributeLink.SETCURRENTKEY(Type,"No.",Sorting);
                   TempAttributeLink.SETRANGE(Type,Type::Template);
                   TempAttributeLink.SETRANGE("No.","No.");
                 END;
               END;

               IF TempAttributeLink.FINDLAST THEN
                 Sorting :=  TempAttributeLink.Sorting + 1
               ELSE
                 Sorting := 1;
             END;

    OnModify=VAR
               WebshopItem@1000000000 : Record 5447304;
             BEGIN
               IF Type=Type::Item THEN
                 WebshopManagement.SetWebshopItemModified("No.",TRUE,FALSE,FALSE);

               IF Type=Type::Category THEN
                 WebshopManagement.SetWebshopCategoryModified("Shop Code","Language Code","Category Line No.");
             END;

    OnDelete=BEGIN
               IF WebshopManagement.ConnectionTest THEN
                 WebshopUpdateManagement.DeleteAttributeLink(Rec);
               AttributeTranslation.SETRANGE(AttributeTranslation.Type,AttributeTranslation.Type::"Attribute Link");
               AttributeTranslation.SETRANGE("Attribute Code","Attribute Code");
               AttributeTranslation.SETRANGE("Attribute Link No.","No.");
               AttributeTranslation.DELETEALL();
             END;

    OnRename=BEGIN
               IF WebshopManagement.ConnectionTest THEN
                 WebshopUpdateManagement.DeleteAttributeLink(xRec);
               AttributeTranslation.SETRANGE(AttributeTranslation.Type,AttributeTranslation.Type::"Attribute Link");
               AttributeTranslation.SETRANGE("Attribute Code",xRec."Attribute Code");
               AttributeTranslation.SETRANGE("Attribute Link No.",xRec."No.");
               AttributeTranslation.DELETEALL();

               IF Rec.Type=Type::Item THEN
                 WebshopManagement.SetWebshopItemModified(Rec."No.",TRUE,FALSE,FALSE);

               IF Type=Type::Category THEN
                 WebshopManagement.SetWebshopCategoryModified("Shop Code","Language Code","Category Line No.");
             END;

    CaptionML=DEU=Webshop Merkmalverkn�pfung;
