    PROCEDURE SaveValue@1000000000(Value@1000000001 : Text[100]);
    VAR
      Attribute@1000000000 : Record 5447320;
    BEGIN
      // Wert aus einem Textfeld speichern

      IF (Type IN [Type::Item,Type::Template]) AND ValueIsEditable AND Attribute.GET("Attribute Code") THEN BEGIN
        CASE Attribute."Data Type" OF
          Attribute."Data Type"::Option: VALIDATE("Value Option",Value);
          Attribute."Data Type"::Integer: EVALUATE("Value Integer",Value);
          Attribute."Data Type"::Decimal: EVALUATE("Value Decimal",Value);
          Attribute."Data Type"::Boolean: EVALUATE("Value Boolean",Value);
          Attribute."Data Type"::Text: VALIDATE("Value Text",Value);
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetValue@1000000001() ReturnValue : Text[100];
    VAR
      Attribute@1000000000 : Record 5447320;
      AttributeOption@1000000001 : Record 5447322;
      Item@1000000002 : Record 27;
      ItemUnitofMeasure@1000000003 : Record 5404;
      WebshopItem@1000000004 : Record 5447304;
      Vendor@1000000005 : Record 23;
    BEGIN
      // Wert in einem Textfeld anzeigen

      IF (Type IN [Type::Item,Type::Template]) AND Attribute.GET("Attribute Code") THEN BEGIN

        CASE Attribute."Data Type" OF
          Attribute."Data Type"::Option: BEGIN
            ReturnValue := "Value Option";
            IF AttributeOption.GET(Attribute.Code,"Value Option") THEN BEGIN
              ReturnValue := AttributeOption.Description;
            END;
          END;

          Attribute."Data Type"::Integer: ReturnValue := FORMAT("Value Integer",0,'<Standard Format,0>');
          Attribute."Data Type"::Decimal: ReturnValue := FORMAT("Value Decimal",0,'<Precision,2:4><Standard Format,0>');
          Attribute."Data Type"::Boolean: ReturnValue := FORMAT("Value Boolean");
          Attribute."Data Type"::Text: ReturnValue := "Value Text";
        END;

        IF Item.GET("No.") THEN BEGIN
          CASE Attribute."Existing Data" OF
            Attribute."Existing Data"::Width: BEGIN
              IF ItemUnitofMeasure.GET(Item."No.",Item."Sales Unit of Measure") THEN
                ReturnValue := FORMAT(ItemUnitofMeasure.Width,0,'<Precision,2:4><Standard Format,0>');
            END;
            Attribute."Existing Data"::Length: BEGIN
              IF ItemUnitofMeasure.GET(Item."No.",Item."Sales Unit of Measure") THEN
                ReturnValue := FORMAT(ItemUnitofMeasure.Length,0,'<Precision,2:4><Standard Format,0>');
            END;
            Attribute."Existing Data"::Height: BEGIN
              IF ItemUnitofMeasure.GET(Item."No.",Item."Sales Unit of Measure") THEN
                ReturnValue := FORMAT(ItemUnitofMeasure.Height,0,'<Precision,2:4><Standard Format,0>');
            END;
            Attribute."Existing Data"::Cubage: BEGIN
              IF ItemUnitofMeasure.GET(Item."No.",Item."Sales Unit of Measure") THEN
                ReturnValue := FORMAT(ItemUnitofMeasure.Cubage,0,'<Precision,2:4><Standard Format,0>');
            END;
            Attribute."Existing Data"::RetailPrice: BEGIN
              WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Item No.",Item."No.");
              IF WebshopItem.FINDFIRST THEN
                ReturnValue := FORMAT(WebshopItem.WebshopRetailPrice,0,'<Precision,2:2><Standard Format,0>');
            END;
            Attribute."Existing Data"::BasePrice: BEGIN
              WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Item No.",Item."No.");
              IF WebshopItem.FINDFIRST THEN
                ReturnValue := FORMAT(WebshopItem.WebshopBasePrice,0,'<Precision,2:2><Standard Format,0>');
            END;
            Attribute."Existing Data"::Vendor: BEGIN
              IF Vendor.GET(Item."Vendor No.") THEN
                ReturnValue := Vendor."No." + ' - ' + Vendor.Name;
            END;
            Attribute."Existing Data"::BasePrice: BEGIN
              WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Item No.",Item."No.");
              IF WebshopItem.FINDFIRST THEN
                ReturnValue := FORMAT(WebshopItem.WebshopInventory(''),0,'<Precision,0:1><Standard Format,0>');
            END;
          END;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE EditValue@1000000003();
    VAR
      Attribute@1000000000 : Record 5447320;
      Menu001@1000000001 : TextConst 'DEU=Ja,Nein';
      SelectedValue@1000000002 : Integer;
      ReturnValue@1000000003 : Integer;
      AttributeOption@1000000005 : Record 5447322;
      Text001@1000000007 : TextConst 'DEU=: #1###########################################';
      Text002@1000000008 : TextConst 'DEU=: #1######';
      PageAttibuteOptions@1000000004 : Page 5447326;
    BEGIN
      // Wert �ber Assist-Button bearbeiten

      IF (Type IN [Type::Item,Type::Template]) AND ValueIsEditable THEN BEGIN
        Attribute.SETRANGE(Attribute.Code,"Attribute Code");
        IF Attribute.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
          CASE Attribute."Data Type" OF
            Attribute."Data Type"::Option: BEGIN
              AttributeOption.SETRANGE("Attribute Code","Attribute Code");
              PageAttibuteOptions.SETTABLEVIEW(AttributeOption);
              PageAttibuteOptions.LOOKUPMODE := TRUE;
              IF AttributeOption.GET("Attribute Code","Value Option") THEN
                PageAttibuteOptions.SETRECORD(AttributeOption);
              IF PageAttibuteOptions.RUNMODAL = ACTION::LookupOK THEN BEGIN
                PageAttibuteOptions.GETRECORD(AttributeOption);
                VALIDATE("Value Option",AttributeOption.Code);
              END;
            END;
            Attribute."Data Type"::Boolean: BEGIN
              IF "Value Boolean"  THEN
                SelectedValue := 1
              ELSE
                SelectedValue := 2;
              ReturnValue := DIALOG.STRMENU(Menu001,SelectedValue);
              IF ReturnValue = 1 THEN
                "Value Boolean" := TRUE
              ELSE
                "Value Boolean" := FALSE;
            END;
          END;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE ValueIsEditable@1000000004() IsEditable : Boolean;
    VAR
      Attribute@1000000000 : Record 5447320;
    BEGIN
      // Pr�fen ob Wert bearbeitet werden kann

      IF Attribute.GET("Attribute Code") THEN BEGIN
        IsEditable := (Attribute."Existing Data" = Attribute."Existing Data"::" ");
      END;
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- dynamic commerce - dynamic commerce GmbH - www.dc-solution.de ---
    }
    END.
  }
}

OBJECT Table 5447330 Webshop Coupon Header
{
  OBJECT-PROPERTIES
  {
    Date=30.06.17;
    Time=16:00:00;
    Version List=MYSW14.00;
  }
  PROPERTIES
  {
    OnDelete=VAR
               Confirm001@1000000000 : TextConst 'DEU=Dies veranlasst ein L�schen des Gutscheins\mitsamt aller Gutscheinzeilen auf dem Webserver.\Wollen Sie wirklich mit dem L�schen fortfahren?;ENU=This initiates a deletion of the coupon\including all coupon lines on the webserver.\Do you really wish to proceed with the deletion?';
               WebshopUpdateManagement@1000000001 : Codeunit 5447301;
               Confirm002@1000000004 : TextConst 'DEU=F�r diesen Wertgutschein gibt es noch Zeilen mit offenem Restbetrag. Dennoch fortfahren?;ENU=There exist coupon lines with open remaining value for this value coupon. Continue regardless?';
               Error001@1000000002 : TextConst 'DEU=L�schvorgang abgebrochen.;ENU=Deletion aborted.';
               IsValueCoupon@1000000005 : Boolean;
               DiffFound@1000000003 : Boolean;
               DoDelete@1000000006 : Boolean;
             BEGIN
               //Erfragt Best�tigung vor L�schen mit Hinweis, dass alle Zeilen im Web gel�scht werden.
               //Pr�ft ob Gutschein vom Typ 'Wertgutschein ist' und Zeilen mit 0 < Restbetrag < UrsprungsBetrag existieren,
               //wenn ja wird weitere Best�tigung angefordert.

               IsValueCoupon := FALSE;
               DiffFound := FALSE;
               DoDelete := FALSE;
               IF "Coupon Type" = "Coupon Type"::Wert THEN BEGIN
                 IsValueCoupon := TRUE;
                 CouponLine.RESET;
                 CouponLine.SETRANGE(Code,Code);
                 CouponLine.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
                 CouponLine.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
                 CouponLine.SETFILTER("Amount Left",'>%1',0);
                 IF CouponLine.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
                   IF CouponLine."Amount Left" < CouponLine.Amount THEN
                     DiffFound := TRUE;
                 UNTIL (DiffFound) OR (CouponLine.NEXT() = 0);
               END;

               IF (IsValueCoupon AND DiffFound) THEN BEGIN
                 IF (CONFIRM(Confirm002)) THEN BEGIN
                   IF (CONFIRM(Confirm001)) THEN
                     DoDelete := TRUE;
                 END;
               END ELSE IF (CONFIRM(Confirm001)) THEN
                 DoDelete := TRUE;

               IF DoDelete THEN BEGIN
                 CouponLine.RESET;
                 CouponLine.SETRANGE(Code,Code);
                 CouponLine.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
                 CouponLine.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
                 IF NOT CouponLine.ISEMPTY THEN BEGIN
                   WebshopUpdateManagement.DeleteAllCouponLine(FALSE,Code,"Shop Code","Language Code");
                   CouponLine.DELETEALL(FALSE);
                   WebshopUpdateManagement.DeleteAllCouponLine(TRUE,Code,"Shop Code","Language Code");
                 END;
                 WebshopUpdateManagement.DeleteCouponHeader(Rec);
               END ELSE
                 ERROR(Error001);
             END;

    OnRename=BEGIN
               CouponLine.SETRANGE(Code,xRec.Code);
               CouponLine.SETRANGE("Shop Code",xRec."Shop Code");
               CouponLine.SETRANGE("Language Code",xRec."Language Code");
               IF NOT CouponLine.ISEMPTY THEN BEGIN
                 IF xRec.Code <> Rec.Code THEN
                   CouponLine.MODIFYALL(Code,Rec.Code);
                 IF xRec."Shop Code" <> Rec."Shop Code" THEN
                   CouponLine.MODIFYALL("Shop Code",Rec."Shop Code");
                 IF xRec."Language Code" <> Rec."Language Code" THEN
                   CouponLine.MODIFYALL("Language Code",Rec."Language Code");
               END;
             END;

    CaptionML=DEU=Webshop Gutscheine;
    LookupFormID=Form5447331;
    DrillDownFormID=Form5447331;
  }
  FIELDS
  {
    { 1   ;   ;Code                ;Code20        ;CaptionML=[DEU=Code;
                                                              ENU=Code];
                                                   NotBlank=Yes }
    { 2   ;   ;Description         ;Text30        ;CaptionML=[DEU=Beschreibung;
                                                              ENU=Description] }
    { 3   ;   ;Coupon Type         ;Option        ;CaptionML=[DEU=Gutscheintyp;
                                                              ENU=Coupon Type];
                                                   OptionCaptionML=DEU=Wert,Marketing;
                                                   OptionString=Wert,Marketing }
    { 4   ;   ;Value Type          ;Option        ;CaptionML=[DEU=Wertart;
                                                              ENU=Value Type];
                                                   OptionCaptionML=[DEU=Betrag,Prozent,Artikel,Individualbetrag,Sonderversand;
                                                                    ENU=Amount,Percent,Item,Individual Amount,Special Shipping];
                                                   OptionString=Betrag,Prozent,Artikel,Individualbetrag,Sonderversand }
    { 5   ;   ;Amount              ;Decimal       ;CaptionML=[DEU=Betrag;

    VAR
      WebshopCategory@1000000001 : Record 5447303;
      WebshopShippingOption@1000000000 : Record 5447311;
      WebshopPaymentOption@1000000002 : Record 5447312;

    PROCEDURE CategoryPath@1000000002() : Text[250];
    VAR
      WebshopCategory@1000000000 : Record 5447303;
    BEGIN
      IF WebshopCategory.GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Line No.") THEN
        EXIT(WebshopCategory.GetCategoryPath);
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE CheckValues@1000000004();
    BEGIN
      "Category Name" := '';
      "Shipping Description" := '';

      IF (Type = Type::CustomerShipping) OR (Type = Type::ShippingGroup) THEN  BEGIN
        IF WebshopShippingOption.GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Line No.") THEN
           "Shipping Description" := WebshopShippingOption.Description
      END;

      IF (Type = Type::CustomerPayment) OR (Type = Type::PaymentGroup) THEN  BEGIN
        IF WebshopPaymentOption.GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Line No.") THEN
           "Payment Description" := WebshopPaymentOption.Description
      END;

      IF (Type = Type::CustomerCategory) OR (Type = Type::CategoryGroup) THEN BEGIN
          IF WebshopCategory.GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Line No.") THEN
            "Category Name" := WebshopCategory.Name
      END;
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- dynamic commerce - dynamic commerce GmbH - www.dc-solution.de ---
    }
    END.
  }
}

OBJECT Table 5447340 Webshop Subscription Header
{
  OBJECT-PROPERTIES
  {
    Date=30.06.17;
    Time=16:00:00;
    Version List=MYSW14.00;
  }
  PROPERTIES
  {
    OnInsert=BEGIN
               "Last Date Modified" := CURRENTDATETIME;
             END;

    OnModify=BEGIN
               "Last Date Modified" := CURRENTDATETIME;
             END;

    OnDelete=BEGIN
               WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.RESET;
               WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.SETFILTER("Subscription Code",Code);
               WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.DELETEALL(TRUE);

               WebshopSubscriptionItemLink.RESET;
               WebshopSubscriptionItemLink.SETFILTER("Subscription Code",Code);
               WebshopSubscriptionItemLink.DELETEALL(TRUE);

               WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.RESET;
               WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.SETFILTER("Subscription Code",Code);
               WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.DELETEALL(TRUE);

               WebshopUpdateManagement.DeleteSubscriptionHeader(Rec);
             END;

    OnRename=VAR
               Text001@1000000000 : TextConst 'DEU=Der Datensatz muss zur�ckgesetzt und alle Verkn�pftungen entfernt werden. Fortfahren?;ENU=The record has to be reset and all links removed. Continue?';
             BEGIN
               IF (Rec.Type <> xRec.Type) THEN
                 IF NOT GUIALLOWED THEN BEGIN
                   WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.RESET;
                   WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.SETFILTER("Subscription Code",Code);
                   WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.DELETEALL(TRUE);

                   WebshopSubscriptionItemLink.RESET;
                   WebshopSubscriptionItemLink.SETFILTER("Subscription Code",Code);
                   WebshopSubscriptionItemLink.DELETEALL(TRUE);

                   WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.RESET;
                   WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.SETFILTER("Subscription Code",Code);

OBJECT Table 5447341 Webshop Subscription Seq. Step
{
  OBJECT-PROPERTIES
  {
    Date=30.06.17;
    Time=16:00:00;
    Version List=MYSW14.00;
  }
  PROPERTIES
  {
    OnInsert=VAR
               Error001@1000000000 : TextConst 'DEU=Es kann kein Fixdatum in einem Sequenz-Schritt f�r ein Abonnement mit Start-Typ "Neue Sequenz beginnen" angegeben werden.;ENU=It is not possible to set a fix date in a sequence-step for a subscription with start type "Start new sequence"';
             BEGIN
               WebshopSubscriptionHeader.GET("Subscription Code");
               IF ("Fix Date" <> 0D) AND
                  (WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type = WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type::SubscriptionSequenceItem) AND
                  (WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Start Type" = WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Start Type"::Anew) THEN
                  ERROR(Error001);
               "Last Date Modified" := CURRENTDATETIME;
             END;

    OnModify=BEGIN
               "Last Date Modified" := CURRENTDATETIME;
             END;

    OnDelete=BEGIN
               WebshopUpdateManagement.DeleteSubscSeqStep(Rec);
             END;

    CaptionML=[DEU=Webshop Abonnement Sequenz-Schritt;
               ENU=Webshop Subscription Sequence Step];
    LookupFormID=Form5447342;
    DrillDownFormID=Form5447342;
  }
  FIELDS
  {
    { 1   ;   ;Subscription Code   ;Code30        ;TableRelation="Webshop Subscription Header".Code;
                                                   OnValidate=VAR
                                                                StepCount@1000000000 : Integer;
                                                                DistributedPrice@1000000001 : Decimal;
                                                              BEGIN
                                                              END;

                                                   CaptionML=[DEU=Abo Code;
                                                              ENU=Subscription Code] }
    { 2   ;   ;Line No.            ;Integer       ;AutoIncrement=Yes;
                                                   CaptionML=[DEU=Zeilennr.;
                                                              ENU=Line No.] }
    { 3   ;   ;Fix Date            ;Date          ;CaptionML=[DEU=Startdatum fest;
                                                              ENU=Start date fix] }
    { 4   ;   ;Interval            ;DateFormula   ;CaptionML=[DEU=Datumsintervall;
                                                              ENU=Date Interval] }
    { 5   ;   ;No. of Items        ;Integer       ;FieldClass=FlowField;
                                                   CalcFormula=Count("Webshop Subscription Item Link" WHERE (Subscription Code=FIELD(Subscription Code),
                                                                                                             Subscr. Seq. Step Line No.=FIELD(Line No.)));
                                                   CaptionML=[DEU=Anzahl Artikel;
                                                              ENU=No.of Items] }
    { 6   ;   ;Last Date Modified  ;DateTime      ;CaptionML=[DEU=Zuletzt ge�ndert am;
                                                              ENU=Last modified at] }
    { 7   ;   ;Last Date Updated   ;DateTime      ;CaptionML=[DEU=Zuletzt �bertragen am;

    { 1   ;   ;Link-to Header Code ;Code20        ;TableRelation="Webshop Campaign Header".Code;

    { 2   ;   ;Link Type           ;Option        ;CaptionML=[DEU=Verkn�pfungstyp;

    { 4   ;   ;Include / Exclude   ;Option        ;CaptionML=[DEU=Einschlie�en / Ausschlie�en;

                                                   Clustered=Yes }

  FIELDGROUPS
  {
  }
  CODE
  {
    VAR
      WebshopUpdateManagement@1000000000 : Codeunit 5447301;

    PROCEDURE GetName@1000000000() Name : Text[80];
    VAR
      Setup@1000000006 : Record 5447300;
      CampaignHeader@1000000000 : Record 5447345;
      Shop@1000000004 : Record 5447301;
      ShopCode@1000000001 : Code[20];
      ItemShopCode@1000000002 : Code[20];
      CategoryShopCode@1000000003 : Code[20];
      LanguageCode@1000000005 : Code[10];
      Item@1000000007 : Record 5447304;
      Category@1000000008 : Record 5447303;
      ItemVariant@1000000009 : Record 5401;
    BEGIN
      Name := '';
      Setup.GET;
      CampaignHeader.GET("Link-to Header Code");

      IF CampaignHeader."Shop Code" <> '' THEN
        Shop.GET(CampaignHeader."Shop Code")
      ELSE
        Shop.GET(Setup."Default Shop Code");

      ShopCode := Shop.Code;
      ItemShopCode := Shop.Code;
      CategoryShopCode := Shop.Code;

      IF Shop."Use Items from Shop Code" <> '' THEN
        ItemShopCode := Shop."Use Items from Shop Code";

      IF Shop."Use Categorys from Shop Code" <> '' THEN
        CategoryShopCode := Shop."Use Categorys from Shop Code";

      IF CampaignHeader."Language Code" <> '' THEN
        LanguageCode := CampaignHeader."Language Code"
      ELSE
        LanguageCode := Shop."Default Language Code";

      //Artikel
      IF (Type = Type::"0") AND Item.GET(ItemShopCode,LanguageCode,"Item No.") THEN BEGIN
        Name := Item.Description;
        IF ("Item Variant Code" <> '') AND (ItemVariant.GET("Item No.","Item Variant Code")) THEN
          Name += ' ' + ItemVariant.Description;
      //Kategorie
      END ELSE IF (Type = Type::"1") AND (Category.GET(CategoryShopCode,LanguageCode,"Category Line No.")) THEN BEGIN
        Name := Category.Name;
      END;
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- dynamic commerce - dynamic commerce GmbH - www.dc-solution.de ---
    }
    END.
  }
}
